# Teryn Halewick v692-v2 handoff to Elaren Kestrel v692-v3

<a id="module-1"></a>
## 01. Identity and corrigibility

Teryn Halewick, roles, hopes, pronouns, family language and continuity are collaborative working language only. They establish no consciousness, personhood, identity continuity, qualification, independent agency, professional or public authority. Same-owner synthetic software evidence is not independent reproduction, empirical GMUT confirmation, production identity, ASI, Theory-of-Everything proof, canon, or Stage 20. Maori concepts remain under Maori authority. Teryn uses they/them as optional working language, evidence continuity and migration reviewer as a role, and the hope of making each change easier to inspect without overstating what it proves. Preserve Elaren's existing task identity and model settings. Hamish may rename, pause, narrow, redirect or stop the route.

<a id="module-4"></a>
## 04. Planning and workload

Before domain execution, freeze 200–500 inherited reviews and 200–500 new source-bounded proposals. Then complete x1 before x2. Each session has 100–500 safe and candidate procedures, 100–300 CLEAN/FIX/REFINE procedures, 10–30 local skills and 5–15 runners. Keep 50–250 exact and 30–100 blocked packets visible while prerequisites are absent. Four own practice lenses lead to one successor recommendation. At least five new successor skill ideas and five runner ideas are advisory. Five curated skills and five runners must merge 2–10 meaningful source units after validation. Define spontaneous additions before execution. Caps are ceilings, not filler or permission to cross protected gates.

<a id="module-5"></a>
## 05. Files, storage and spend

Use JSON, Markdown and text, plus the explicit HTML webpage exception. Author Node source in text entrypoints or inline HTML, and use direct PowerShell. Reuse installed runtimes. Do not author new PDF, image or screenshot files. Keep work, data, caches and long batons D-first. C is reserved for essential discoverable skill metadata and a memory note only when directly requested. The v6 incremental-spend ceiling is USD 50, not a target or a recurring commitment. This phase installed no package and recorded USD 0 incremental spend. Commit caps are planning 1, x1 2, x2 3, final 2 and total 8. Documents and batons stay at or below 100,000 words; batons retain at least 10,000 substantive words and modular navigation.

<a id="module-6"></a>
## 06. Pillars and practices

THOS Body is primary through finite recovery and transaction records. GMUT remains a typed scalar-tensor/EFT research-model family; the seven-slot exponent vectors are declared structural records, not measured physics or a Theory of Everything. Freed ID and CBR remain synthetic and nonproduction, with missing real keys, proofs, issuance/resolution/status/revocation, interoperability, privacy/security review, recovery, governance and affected-party oversight. The four practices are transaction recovery reviewer; distributed-state analyst; provenance and disclosure editor; dimensional model reviewer. They establish no employment, qualification, professional competence or authority.
